\"\"\"Creates an LLM engine from the engine arguments.\"\"\"\n   125→        # Create the engine configs.\n   126→        engine_configs = engine_args.create_engine_configs()\n   127→        parallel_config = engine_configs[2]\n   128→        device_config = engine_configs[4]\n   129→\n   130→        # Initialize the cluster and specify the executor class.\n   131→        if executor_class = RayGPUExecutor\n   138→        else:\n   139→            assert parallel_config.world_size == 1, (\n   140→                \"Ray is required if parallel_config.world_size > 1.\")\n   141→            from vllm.executor.gpu_executor import GPUExecutor\n   142→            executor_class = GPUExecutor\n   143→\n   144→        # Create the LLM engine.\n   145→        engine = cls(*engine_configs,\n   146→                     executor_class=executor_class,\n   147→                     log_stats=not engine_args.disable_log_stats)\n   148→        return engine\n   149→\n   150→    def __reduce__(self):\n   151→        # This is to ensure that the LLMEngine is not referenced in\n   152→        # the closure used to initialize Ray worker actors\n   153→        raise RuntimeError(\"LLMEngine should not be pickled!\")\n   154→\n   155→    def get_tokenizer(self) -> \"PreTrainedTokenizer\":\n   156→        return self.tokenizer.get_lora_tokenizer()\n   157→\n   158→    def get_tokenizer_for_seq(self,\n   159→                              sequence: Sequence) -> \"PreTrainedTokenizer\":\n   160→        return        self.parallel_config.tokenizer_pool_config, **init_kwargs)\n   175→\n   176→        if len(self.get_tokenizer()) != self.model_config.get_vocab_size():\n   177→            logger.warning(\n The exact scheduling policy is\n   219→        determined by the scheduler.\n   220→\n   221→        Args:\n   222→            request_id: The unique ID of the request.\n   223→            prompt: The prompt string. Can be None if prompt_token_ids is\n   224→                provided.\n   225→            sampling_params: The sampling parameters for text generation.\n   226→            prompt_token_ids: The token IDs of the prompt. If None, we\n   227→                use the tokenizer to convert the prompts to token IDs.\n   228→            arrival_time: The arrival time of the request. If None, we use\n   229→                the current monotonic time.\n   230→\n   231→        Details:\n   232→            - Set arrival_time to the current time if it is None.\n   233→            - Set prompt_token_ids to the encoded prompt if it is None.\n   234→            - Create `best_of` number of :class:`~vllm.Sequence` objects.\n   235→            - Create a :class:`~vllm.SequenceGroup` object\n   236→              from the list of :class:`~vllm.Sequence`.\n   237→            - Add the :class:`~vllm.SequenceGroup` object to the scheduler.\n   238→\n   239→        Example:\n   240→            >>> # initialize engine\n   241→            >>> engine = LLMEngine.from_engine_args(engine_args)\n   242→            >>> # set request arguments\n   243→            >>> example_prompt = \"Who is the president of the United States?\"\n   244→            >>> sampling_params = SamplingParams(temperature=0.0)\n   245→            >>> request_id = 0\n   246→            >>>\n   247→            >>> # add the request to the engine\n   248→            >>> engine.add_request(\n   249→            >>>    str(request_id),\n   250→            >>>    example_prompt,\n   251→            >>>    SamplingParams(temperature=0.0))\n   252→            >>> # continue the request processing\n   253→            >>> ...\n   254→        \"\"\"\n   255→        if lora_request is not None and not self.lora_config:\n   256→            raise ValueError(f\"Got lora_request {lora_request} but LoRA is \"\n   257→                             \"not enabled!\")\n   258→        max_logprobs = self.get_model_config().max_logprobs\n   259→        if (sampling_params.logprobs\n   260→                and sampling_params.logprobs > max_logprobs) or (\n   261→                    sampling_params.prompt_logprobs\n   262→                    and sampling_params.prompt_logprobs > max_logprobs):\n   263→            raise ValueError(f\"Cannot request more than \"\n   264→                             f\"{max_logprobs} logprobs.\")\n   265→        if arrival_time is None:\n   266→            arrival_time = time.time()\n   267→        prompt_token_ids = self.encode_request(\n   268→            request_id=request_id,\n   269→            prompt=prompt,\n   270→            prompt_token_ids=prompt_token_ids,\n   271→            lora_request=lora_request)\n   272→\n   273→        # Create the sequences.\n   274→        block_size = self.cache_config.block_size\n   275→        seq_id = next(self.seq_counter)\n   276→        eos_token_id = self.tokenizer.get_lora_tokenizer(\n   277→            lora_request).eos_token_id\n   278→        seq = Sequence(seq_id, prompt, prompt_token_ids, block_size,\n   279→                       eos_token_id, lora_request)\n   280→\n   281→        # Defensive copy of SamplingParams, which are used by the sampler,\n   282→        # this doesn't deep-copy LogitsProcessor objects\n   283→        sampling_params = sampling_params.clone()\n   284→\n   285→        # Create the sequence group.\n   286→        seq_group = SequenceGroup(request_id, [seq], sampling_params,\n   287→                                  arrival_time, lora_request)\n   288→\n   289→        # Add the sequence group to the scheduler.\n   290→        self.scheduler.add_seq_group(seq_group)\n   291→\n   292→    def abort_request(self, request_id: Union[str, Iterable[str]]) -> None:\n   293→        \"\"\"Aborts a request(s) with the given ID.\n   294→\n   295→        Args:\n   296→            request_id: The ID(s) of the request to abort.\n   297→\n   298→        Details:\n   299→            - Refer to the\n   300→              :meth:`~vllm.core.scheduler.Scheduler.abort_seq_group`\n   301→              from class :class:`~vllm.core.scheduler.Scheduler`.\n   302→\n   303→        Example:\n   304→            >>> # initialize engine and add a request with request_id\n   305→            >>> request_id = str(0)\n   306→            >>> # abort the request\n   307→            >>> engine.abort_request(request_id)\n   308→        \"\"\"\n   309→        self.scheduler.abort_seq_group(request_id)\n   310→\n   311→    def get_model_config(self) -> ModelConfig:\n   312→        \"\"\"Gets the model configuration.\"\"\"\n   313→        return self.model_config\n   314→\n   315→    def get_num_unfinished_requests(self) -> int:\n   316→        \"\"\"Gets the number of unfinished requests.\"\"\"\n   317→        return self.scheduler.get_num_unfinished_seq_groups()\n   318→\n   319→    def has_unfinished_requests(self) -> bool:\n   320→        \"\"\"Returns True if there are unfinished requests.\"\"\"\n   321→        return self.scheduler.has_unfinished_seqs()\n   322→\n   323→    def _check_beam_search_early_stopping(\n   324→        self,\n   325→        early_stopping: Union[bool, str],\n   326→        sampling_params: SamplingParams,\n   327→        best_running_seq: Sequence,\n   328→        current_worst_seq: Sequence,\n   329→    ) -> bool:\n   330→        assert sampling_params.use_beam_search\n   331→        length_penalty = sampling_params.length_penalty\n   332→        if early_stopping is True:\n   333→            return True\n   334→\n   335→        current_worst_score = current_worst_seq.get_beam_search_score(\n   336→            length_penalty=length_penalty,\n   337→            eos_token_id=current_worst_seq.eos_token_id)\n   338→        if early_stopping is False:\n   339→            highest_attainable_score = best_running_seq.get_beam_search_score(\n   340→                length_penalty=length_penalty,\n   341→                eos_token_id=best_running_seq.eos_token_id)\n   342→        else:\n   343→            assert early_stopping == \"never\"\n   344→            if length_penalty > 0.0:\n   345→                # If length_penalty > 0.0, beam search will prefer longer\n   346→                # sequences. The highest attainable score calculation is\n   347→                # based on the longest possible sequence length in this case.\n   348→                max_possible_length = max(\n   349→                    best_running_seq.get_prompt_len() +\n   350→                    sampling_params.max_tokens,\n   351→                    self.scheduler_config.max_model_len)\n   352→                highest_attainable_score = (\n   353→                    best_running_seq.get_beam_search_score(\n   354→                        length_penalty=length_penalty,\n   355→                        eos_token_id=best_running_seq.eos_token_id,\n   356→                        seq_len=max_possible_length))\n   357→            else:\n   358→                # Otherwise, beam search will prefer shorter sequences. The\n   359→                # highest attainable score calculation is based on the current\n   360→                # sequence length.\n   361→                highest_attainable_score = (\n   362→                    best_running_seq.get_beam_search_score(\n   363→                        length_penalty=length_penalty,\n   364→                        eos_token_id=best_running_seq.eos_token_id))\n   365→        return current_worst_score >= highest_attainable_score\n   366→\n   367→    def _process_sequence_group_outputs(self, seq_group: SequenceGroup,\n   368→                                        outputs: SequenceGroupOutput) -> None:\n   369→\n   370→        # Process prompt logprobs\n   371→        prompt_logprobs = outputs.prompt_logprobs\n   372→        if prompt_logprobs is not None:\n   373→            # We can pick any sequence for the prompt.\n   374→            seq = next(iter(seq_group.seqs_dict.values()))\n   375→            all_token_ids = seq.get_token_ids()\n   376→            for i, prompt_logprobs_for_token in enumerate(prompt_logprobs):\n   377→                self._decode_logprobs(seq, seq_group.sampling_params,\n   378→                                      prompt_logprobs_for_token,\n   379→                                      all_token_ids[:i])\n   380→            seq_group.prompt_logprobs = prompt_logprobs\n   381→\n   382→        # Process samples\n   383→        samples = outputs.samples\n   384→        parent_seqs = seq_group.get_seqs(status=SequenceStatus.RUNNING)\n   385→        existing_finished_seqs = seq_group.get_finished_seqs()\n   386→        parent_child_dict = {\n   387→            parent_seq.seq_id: []\n   388→            for parent_seq in parent_seqs\n   389→        }\n   390→        for sample in samples:\n   391→            parent_child_dict[sample.parent_seq_id].append(sample)\n   392→        # List of (child, parent)\n   393→        child_seqs: List[Tuple[Sequence, Sequence]] = []\n   394→\n   395→        # Process the child samples for each parent sequence\n   396→        for parent in parent_seqs:\n   397→            child_samples: List[SequenceOutput] = parent_child_dict[\n   398→                parent.seq_id]\n   399→            if len(child_samples) == 0:\n   400→                # This parent sequence has no children samples. Remove\n   401→                # the parent sequence from the sequence group since it will\n   402→                # not be used in the future iterations.\n   403→                parent.status = SequenceStatus.FINISHED_ABORTED\n   404→                seq_group.remove(parent.seq_id)\n   405→                self.scheduler.free_seq(parent)\n   406→                continue\n   407→            # Fork the parent sequence if there are multiple child samples.\n   408→            for child_sample in child_samples[:-1]:\n   409→                new_child_seq_id = next(self.seq_counter)\n   410→                child = parent.fork(new_child_seq_id)\n   411→                child.append_token_id(child_sample.output_token,\n   412→                                      child_sample.logprobs)\n   413→                child_seqs.append((child, parent))\n   414→            # Continue the parent sequence for the last child sample.\n   415→            # We reuse the parent sequence here to reduce redundant memory\n   416→            # copies, especially when using non-beam search sampling methods.\n   417→            last_child_sample = child_samples[-1]\n   418→            parent.append_token_id(last_child_sample.output_token,\n   419→                                   last_child_sample.logprobs)\n   420→            child_seqs.append((parent, parent))\n   421→\n   422→        for seq, _ in child_seqs:\n   423→            self._decode_sequence(seq, seq_group.sampling_params)\n   424→            self._check_stop(seq, seq_group.sampling_params)\n   425→\n   426→        # Non-beam search case\n   427→        if not seq_group.sampling_params.use_beam_search:\n   428→            # For newly created child sequences, add them to the sequence group\n   429→            # and fork them in block manager if they are not finished.\n   430→            for seq, parent in child_seqs:\n   431→                if seq is not parent:\n   432→                    seq_group.add(seq)\n   433→                    if not seq.is_finished():\n   434→                        self.scheduler.fork_seq(parent, seq)\n   435→\n   436→            # Free the finished and selected parent sequences' memory in block\n   437→            # manager. Keep them in the sequence group as candidate output.\n   438→            # NOTE: we need to fork the new sequences before freeing the\n   439→            # old sequences.\n   440→            for seq, parent in child_seqs:\n   441→                if seq is parent and seq.is_finished():\n   442→                    self.scheduler.free_seq(seq)\n   443→            return\n   444→\n   445→        # Beam search case\n   446→        # Select the child sequences to keep in the sequence group.\n   447→        selected_child_seqs = []\n   448→        unselected_child_seqs = []\n   449→        beam_width = seq_group.sampling_params.best_of\n   450→        length_penalty = seq_group.sampling_params.length_penalty\n   451→\n   452→        # Select the newly finished sequences with the highest scores\n   453→        # to replace existing finished sequences.\n   454→        # Tuple of (seq, parent, is_new)\n   455→        existing_finished_seqs = [(seq, None, False)\n   456→                                  for seq in existing_finished_seqs]\n   457→        new_finished_seqs = [(seq, parent, True) for seq, parent in child_seqs\n   458→                             if seq.is_finished()]\n   459→        all_finished_seqs = existing_finished_seqs + new_finished_seqs\n   460→        # Sort the finished sequences by their scores.\n   461→        all_finished_seqs.sort(key=lambda x: x[0].get_beam_search_score(\n   462→            length_penalty=length_penalty, eos_token_id=x[0].eos_token_id),\n   463→                               reverse=True)\n   464→        for seq, parent, is_new in all_finished_seqs[:beam_width]:\n   465→            if is_new:\n   466→                # A newly generated child sequence finishes and has a high\n   467→                # score, so we will add it into the sequence group.\n   468→                selected_child_seqs.append((seq, parent))\n   469→        for seq, parent, is_new in all_finished_seqs[beam_width:]:\n   470→            if is_new:\n   471→                # A newly generated child sequence finishes but has a low\n   472→                # score, so we will not add it into the sequence group.\n   473→                # Additionally, if this sequence is a continuation of a\n   474→                # parent sequence, we will need remove the parent sequence\n   475→                # from the sequence group.\n   476→                unselected_child_seqs.append((seq, parent))\n   477→            else:\n   478→                # An existing finished sequence has a low score, so we will\n   479→                # remove it from the sequence group.\n   480→                seq_group.remove(seq.seq_id)\n   481→\n   482→        # select the top beam_width sequences from the running\n   483→        # sequences for the next iteration to continue the beam\n   484→        # search.\n   485→        running_child_seqs = [(seq, parent) for seq, parent in child_seqs\n   486→                              if not seq.is_finished()]\n   487→        # Sort the running sequences by their scores.\n   488→        running_child_seqs.sort(key=lambda x: x[0].get_beam_search_score(\n   489→            length_penalty=length_penalty, eos_token_id=x[0].eos_token_id),\n   490→                                reverse=True)\n   491→\n   492→        # Check if we can stop the beam search.\n   493→        if len(running_child_seqs) == 0:\n   494→            # No running sequences, stop the beam search.\n   495→            stop_beam_search = True\n   496→        elif len(all_finished_seqs) < beam_width:\n   497→            # Not enough finished sequences, continue the beam search.\n   498→            stop_beam_search = False\n   499→        else:\n   500→            # Check the early stopping criteria\n   501→            best_running_seq = running_child_seqs[0][0]\n   502→            current_worst_seq = all_finished_seqs[beam_width - 1][0]\n   503→            stop_beam_search = self._check_beam_search_early_stopping(\n   504→                seq_group.sampling_params.early_stopping,\n   505→                seq_group.sampling_params, best_running_seq, current_worst_seq)\n   506→\n   507→        if stop_beam_search:\n   508→            # Stop the beam search and remove all the running sequences from\n   509→            # the sequence group.\n   510→            unselected_child_seqs.extend(running_child_seqs)\n   511→        else:\n   512→            # Continue the beam search and select the top beam_width sequences\n   513→            # to continue the beam search.\n   514→            selected_child_seqs.extend(running_child_seqs[:beam_width])\n   515→            # The remaining running sequences will not be used in the next\n   516→            # iteration. Again, if these sequences are continuations of\n   517→            # parent sequences, we will need to remove the parent sequences\n   518→            # from the sequence group.\n   519→            unselected_child_seqs.extend(running_child_seqs[beam_width:])\n   520→\n   521→        # For newly created child sequences, add them to the sequence group\n   522→        # and fork them in block manager if they are not finished.\n   523→        for seq, parent in selected_child_seqs:\n   524→            if seq is not parent:\n   525→                seq_group.add(seq)\n   526→                if not seq.is_finished():\n   527→                    self.scheduler.fork_seq(parent, seq)\n   528→\n   529→        # Free the finished and selected parent sequences' memory in block\n   530→        # manager. Keep them in the sequence group as candidate output.\n   531→        for seq, parent in selected_child_seqs:\n   532→            if seq is parent and seq.is_finished():\n   533→                self.scheduler.free_seq(seq)\n   534→\n   535→        # Remove the unselected parent sequences from the sequence group and\n   536→        # free their memory in block manager.\n   537→        for seq, parent in unselected_child_seqs:\n   538→            if seq is parent:\n   539→                # Remove the parent sequence if it is not selected for next\n   540→                # iteration\n   541→                seq_group.remove(seq.seq_id)\n   542→                self.scheduler.free_seq(seq)\n   543→\n   544→    def _process_model_outputs(\n   545→            self, output: SamplerOutput,\n   546→            scheduler_outputs: SchedulerOutputs) -> List[RequestOutput]:\n   547→        now = time.time()\n   548→        # Update the scheduled sequence groups with the model outputs.\n   549→        scheduled_seq_groups = scheduler_outputs.scheduled_seq_groups\n   550→\n   551→        # If prefix caching is enabled, mark all blocks in the sequence groups\n   552→        # If length_penalty > 0.0, beam search will prefer longer\n                # sequences. The highest attainable score calculation is\n                # based on the longest possible sequence length in this case.\n                max_possible_length = max(\n                    best_running_seq.get_prompt_len() +\n                    sampling_params.max_tokens,\n                    self.scheduler_config.max_model_len)\n                highest_attainable_score = (\n                    best_running_seq.get_beam_search_score(\n                        length_penalty=length_penalty,\n                        eos_token_id=best_running_seq.eos_token_id,\n                        seq_len=max_possible_length))\n            else:\n                # Otherwise, beam search will prefer shorter sequences. The\n                # highest attainable score calculation is based on the current\n                # sequence length.\n                highest_attainable_score = (\n                    best_running_seq.get_beam_search_score(\n                        length_penalty=length_penalty,\n                        eos_token_id=best_running_seq.eos_token_id))\n        return current_worst_score >= highest_attainable_score\n\n    def _process_sequence_group_outputs(self, seq_group: SequenceGroup,\n                                        outputs: SequenceGroupOutput) -> None:\n\n        # Process prompt logprobs\n        prompt_logprobs = outputs.prompt_logprobs\n        if prompt_logprobs is not None:\n            # We can pick any sequence for the prompt.\n            seq = next(iter(seq_group.seqs_dict.values()))\n            all_token_ids = seq.get_token_ids()\n            for i, prompt_logprobs_for_token in enumerate(prompt_logprobs):\n                self._decode_logprobs(seq, seq_group.sampling_params,\n                                      prompt_logprobs_for_token,\n                                      all_token_ids[:i])\n            seq_group.prompt_logprobs = prompt_logprobs\n\n        # Process samples\n        samples = outputs.samples\n        parent_seqs = seq_group.get_seqs(status=SequenceStatus.RUNNING)\n        existing_finished_seqs = seq_group.get_finished_seqs()\n        parent_child_dict = {\n            parent_seq.seq_id: []\n            for parent_seq in parent_seqs\n        }\n        for sample in samples:\n            parent_child_dict[sample.parent_seq_id].append(sample)\n        # List of (child, parent)\n        child_seqs: List[Tuple[Sequence, Sequence]] = []\n\n        # Process the child samples for each parent sequence\n        for parent in parent_seqs:\n            child_samples: List[SequenceOutput] = parent_child_dict[\n                parent.seq_id]\n            if len(child_samples) == 0:\n                # This parent sequence has no children samples. Remove\n                # the parent sequence from the sequence group since it will\n                # not be used in the future iterations.\n                parent.status = SequenceStatus.FINISHED_ABORTED\n                seq_group.remove(parent.seq_id)\n                self.scheduler.free_seq(parent)\n                continue\n            # Fork the parent sequence if there are multiple child samples.\n            for child_sample in child_samples[:-1]:\n                new_child_seq_id = next(self.seq_counter)\n                child = parent.fork(new_child_seq_id)\n                child.append_token_id(child_sample.output_token,\n                                      child_sample.logprobs)\n                child_seqs.append((child, parent))\n            # Continue the parent sequence for the last child sample.\n            # We reuse the parent sequence here to reduce redundant memory\n            # copies, especially when using non-beam search sampling methods.\n            last_child_sample = child_samples[-1]\n            parent.append_token_id(last_child_sample.output_token,\n                                   last_child_sample.logprobs)\n            child_seqs.append((parent, parent))\n\n        for seq, _ in child_seqs:\n            self._decode_sequence(seq, seq_group.sampling_params)\n            self._check_stop(seq, seq_group.sampling_params)\n\n        # Non-beam search case\n        if not seq_group.sampling_params.use_beam_search:\n            # For newly created child sequences, add them to the sequence group\n            # and fork them in block manager if they are not finished.\n            for seq, parent in child_seqs:\n                if seq is not parent:\n                    seq_group.add(seq)\n                    if not seq.is_finished():\n                        self.scheduler.fork_seq(parent, seq)\n\n            # Free the finished and selected parent sequences' memory in block\n            # manager. Keep them in the sequence group as candidate output.\n            # NOTE: we need to fork the new sequences before freeing the\n            # old sequences.\n            for seq, parent in child_seqs:\n                if seq is parent and seq.is_finished():\n                    self.scheduler.free_seq(seq)\n            return\n\n        # Beam search case\n        # Select the child sequences to keep in the sequence group.\n        selected_child_seqs = []\n        unselected_child_seqs = []\n        beam_width = seq_group.sampling_params.best_of\n        length_penalty = seq_group.sampling_params.length_penalty\n\n        # Select the newly finished sequences with the highest scores\n        # to replace existing finished sequences.\n        # Tuple of (seq, parent, is_new)\n        existing_finished_seqs = [(seq, None, False)\n                                  for seq in existing_finished_seqs]\n        new_finished_seqs = [(seq, parent, True) for seq, parent in child_seqs\n                             if seq.is_finished()]\n        all_finished_seqs = existing_finished_seqs + new_finished_seqs\n        # Sort the finished sequences by their scores.\n        all_finished_seqs.sort(key=lambda x: x[0].get_beam_search_score(\n            length_penalty=length_penalty, eos_token_id=x[0].eos_token_id),\n                               reverse=True)\n        for seq, parent, is_new in all_finished_seqs[:beam_width]:\n            if is_new:\n                # A newly generated child sequence finishes and has a high\n                # score, so we will add it into the sequence group.\n                selected_child_seqs.append((seq, parent))\n        for seq, parent, is_new in all_finished_seqs[beam_width:]:\n            if is_new:\n                # A newly generated child sequence finishes but has a low\n                # score, so we will not add it into the sequence group.\n                # Additionally, if this sequence is a continuation of a\n                # parent sequence, we will need remove the parent sequence\n                # from the sequence group.\n                unselected_child_seqs.append((seq, parent))\n            else:\n                # An existing finished sequence has a low score, so we will\n                # remove it from the sequence group.\n                seq_group.remove(seq.seq_id)\n\n        # select the top beam_width sequences from the running\n        # sequences for the next iteration to continue the beam\n        # search.\n        running_child_seqs = [(seq, parent) for seq, parent in child_seqs\n                              if not seq.is_finished()]\n        # Sort the running sequences by their scores.\n        running_child_seqs.sort(key=lambda x: x[0].get_beam_search_score(\n            length_penalty=length_penalty, eos_token_id=x[0].eos_token_id),\n                                reverse=True)\n\n        # Check if we can stop the beam search.\n        if len(running_child_seqs) == 0:\n            # No running sequences, stop the beam search.\n            stop_beam_search = True\n        elif len(all_finished_seqs) < beam_width:\n            # Not enough finished sequences, continue the beam search.\n            stop_beam_search = False\n        else:\n            # Check the early stopping criteria\n            best_running_seq = running_child_seqs[0][0]\n            current_worst_seq = all_finished_seqs[beam_width - 1][0]\n            stop_beam_search = self._check_beam_search_early_stopping(\n                seq_group.sampling_params.early_stopping,\n                seq_group.sampling_params, best_running_seq, current_worst_seq)\n\n        if stop_beam_search:\n            # Stop the beam search and remove all the running sequences from\n            # the sequence group.\n            unselected_child_seqs.extend(running_child_seqs)\n        else:\n            # Continue the beam search and select the top beam_width sequences\n            # to continue the beam search.\n            selected_child_seqs.extend(running_child_seqs[:beam_width])\n            # The remaining running sequences will not be used in the next\n            # iteration. Again, if these sequences are continuations of\n            # parent sequences, we will need to remove the parent sequences\n            # from the sequence group.\n            unselected_child_seqs.extend(running_child_seqs[beam_width:])\n\n        # For newly created child sequences, add them to the sequence group\n        # and fork them in block manager if they are not finished.\n        for seq, parent in selected_child_seqs:\n            if seq is not parent:\n                seq_group.add(seq)\n                if not seq.is_finished():\n                    self.scheduler.fork_seq(parent, seq)\n\n        # Free the finished and selected parent sequences' memory in block\n        # manager. Keep them in the sequence group as candidate output.\n        for seq, parent in selected_child_seqs:\n            if seq is parent and seq.is_finished():\n                self.scheduler.free_seq(seq)\n\n        # Remove the unselected parent sequences from the sequence group and\n        # free their memory in block manager.\n        for seq, parent in unselected_child_seqs:\n            if seq is parent:\n                # Remove the parent sequence if it is not selected for next\n                # iteration\n                seq_group.remove(seq.seq_id)\n                self.scheduler.free_seq(seq)\n\n    def _process_model_outputs(\n            self, output: SamplerOutput,\n            scheduler_outputs: SchedulerOutputs) -> List[RequestOutput]:\n        now = time.time()\n        # Update the scheduled sequence groups with the model outputs.\n        scheduled_seq_groups = scheduler_outputs.scheduled_seq_groups\n\n        # If prefix caching is enabled, mark all blocks in the sequence groups\n        # as completed so that future requests don't attempt to recompute them\n        if self.cache_config.enable_prefix_caching:\n            for seq_group in scheduled_seq_groups:\n                self.scheduler.mark_blocks_as_computed(seq_group)\n\n        for seq_group, outputs in zip(scheduled_seq_groups, output):\n            self._process_sequence_group_outputs(seq_group, outputs)\n\n        # parameters` (`use_beam_search` or not).\n                - Frees the finished sequence groups.\n\n            - Finally, it creates and returns the newly generated results.\n\n        Example:\n Logged to Prometheus.\"\"\"\n        now = time.time()\n\n        # KV Cache Usage in %.\n        num_total_gpu = self.cache_config.num_gpu_blocks\n        num_free_gpu = self.scheduler.block_manager.get_num_free_gpu_blocks()\n        gpu_cache_usage = 1.0 - (num_free_gpu / num_total_gpu)\n\n        num_total_cpu = self.cache_config.num_cpu_blocks\n It includes a tokenizer, a\n    language model (possibly distributed across multiple GPUs), and GPU memory\n    space allocated for intermediate states (aka KV cache). This class utilizes\n    iteration-level scheduling and efficient memory management to maximize the\n    serving throughput.\n\n    The `LLM` class wraps this class for offline batched inference and the\n    `AsyncLLMEngine` class wraps this class for online serving.\n\n    NOTE: The config arguments are derived from the `EngineArgs` class. pickled!\")\n\n    def get_tokenizer(self) -> \"PreTrainedTokenizer\":\n        return self.tokenizer.get_lora_tokenizer()\n\n    def get_tokenizer_for_seq(self,\n The exact scheduling policy is\n        determined by the scheduler.\n\n        Args:\n            request_id: The unique ID of the request.\n            prompt: The prompt string. Can be None if prompt_token_ids is\n                provided.\n            sampling_params: The sampling parameters for text generation.\n            prompt_token_ids: The token IDs of the prompt. If None, we\n                use the tokenizer to convert the prompts to token IDs.\n            arrival_time: The arrival time of the request. If None, we use\n                the current monotonic time.\n\n        Details:\n            - Set arrival_time to the current time if it is None.\n            - Set prompt_token_ids to the encoded prompt if it is None.\n            - Create `best_of` number of :class:`~vllm.Sequence` objects.\n            - Create a :class:`~vllm.SequenceGroup` object\n              from the list of :class:`~vllm.Sequence`.\n            - Add the :class:`~vllm.SequenceGroup` object to the scheduler.\n\n        Example:\n            >>> # initialize engine\n            >>> engine = LLMEngine.from_engine_args(engine_args)\n            >>> # set request arguments\n            >>> example_prompt = \"Who is the president of the United States?\"\n            >>> sampling_params = SamplingParams(temperature=0.0)\n            >>> request_id = 0\n            >>>\n            >>> # add the request to the engine\n            >>> engine.add_request(\n            >>>    str(request_id),\n            >>>    example_prompt,\n            >>>    SamplingParams(temperature=0.0))\n            >>> # continue the request processing\n            >>> ...\n        \"\"\"\n        if lora_request is not None and not self.lora_config:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Got lora_request {lora_request} but LoRA is \"\n                             \"not enabled!\")\n        max_logprobs = self.get_model_config().max_logprobs\n        if (sampling_params.logprobs\n                and sampling_params.logprobs > max_logprobs) or (\n                    sampling_params.prompt_logprobs\n                    and sampling_params.prompt_logprobs > max_logprobs):\n            raise ValueError(f\"Cannot request more than \"\n                             f\"{max_logprobs} logprobs.\")\n        if arrival_time is None:\n            arrival_time = time.time()\n        prompt_token_ids = self.encode_request(\n            request_id=request_id,\n            prompt=prompt,\n            prompt_token_ids=prompt_token_ids,\n            lora_request=lora_request)\n\n        # If length_penalty > 0.0, beam search will prefer longer\n                # sequences. The highest attainable score calculation is\n                # based on the longest possible sequence length in this case.\n                max_possible_length = max(\n                    best_running_seq.get_prompt_len() +\n                    sampling_params.max_tokens,\n                    self.scheduler_config.max_model_len)\n                highest_attainable_score = (\n                    best_running_seq.get_beam_search_score(\n                        length_penalty=length_penalty,\n                        eos_token_id=best_running_seq.eos_token_id,\n                        seq_len=max_possible_length))\n            else:\n                # Otherwise, beam search will prefer shorter sequences. The\n                # highest attainable score calculation is based on the current\n                # sequence length.\n                highest_attainable_score = (\n                    best_running_seq.get_beam_search_score(\n                        length_penalty=length_penalty,\n                        eos_token_id=best_running_seq.eos_token_id))\n        return current_worst_score >= highest_attainable_score\n\n    def _process_sequence_group_outputs(self, seq_group: SequenceGroup,\n                                        outputs: SequenceGroupOutput) -> None:\n\n        # Process prompt logprobs\n        prompt_logprobs = outputs.prompt_logprobs\n        if prompt_logprobs is not None:\n            # We can pick any sequence for the prompt.\n            seq = next(iter(seq_group.seqs_dict.values()))\n            all_token_ids = seq.get_token_ids()\n            for i, prompt_logprobs_for_token in enumerate(prompt_logprobs):\n                self._decode_logprobs(seq, seq_group.sampling_params,\n                                      prompt_logprobs_for_token,\n                                      all_token_ids[:i])\n            seq_group.prompt_logprobs = prompt_logprobs\n\n        # Process samples\n        samples = outputs.samples\n        parent_seqs = seq_group.get_seqs(status=SequenceStatus.RUNNING)\n        existing_finished_seqs = seq_group.get_finished_seqs()\n        parent_child_dict = {\n            parent_seq.seq_id: []\n            for parent_seq in parent_seqs\n        }\n        for sample in samples:\n            parent_child_dict[sample.parent_seq_id].append(sample)\n        # List of (child, parent)\n        child_seqs: List[Tuple[Sequence, Sequence]] = []\n\n        # Process the child samples for each parent sequence\n        for parent in parent_seqs:\n            child_samples: List[SequenceOutput] = parent_child_dict[\n                parent.seq_id]\n            if len(child_samples) == 0:\n                # This parent sequence has no children samples. Remove\n                # the parent sequence from the sequence group since it will\n                # not be used in the future iterations.\n                parent.status = SequenceStatus.FINISHED_ABORTED\n                seq_group.remove(parent.seq_id)\n                self.scheduler.free_seq(parent)\n                continue\n            # Fork the parent sequence if there are multiple child samples.\n            for child_sample in child_samples[:-1]:\n                new_child_seq_id = next(self.seq_counter)\n                child = parent.fork(new_child_seq_id)\n                child.append_token_id(child_sample.output_token,\n                                      child_sample.logprobs)\n                child_seqs.append((child, parent))\n            # Continue the parent sequence for the last child sample.\n            # We reuse the parent sequence here to reduce redundant memory\n            # copies, especially when using non-beam search sampling methods.\n            last_child_sample = child_samples[-1]\n            parent.append_token_id(last_child_sample.output_token,\n                                   last_child_sample.logprobs)\n            child_seqs.append((parent, parent))\n\n        for seq, _ in child_seqs:\n            self._decode_sequence(seq, seq_group.sampling_params)\n            self._check_stop(seq, seq_group.sampling_params)\n\n        # Non-beam search case\n        if not seq_group.sampling_params.use_beam_search:\n            # For newly created child sequences, add them to the sequence group\n            # and fork them in block manager if they are not finished.\n            for seq, parent in child_seqs:\n                if seq is not parent:\n                    seq_group.add(seq)\n                    if not seq.is_finished():\n                        self.scheduler.fork_seq(parent, seq)\n\n            # Free the finished and selected parent sequences' memory in block\n            # manager. Keep them in the sequence group as candidate output.\n            # NOTE: we need to fork the new sequences before freeing the\n            # old sequences.\n            for seq, parent in child_seqs:\n                if seq is parent and seq.is_finished():\n                    self.scheduler.free_seq(seq)\n            return\n\n        # Beam search case\n        # Select the child sequences to keep in the sequence group.\n        selected_child_seqs = []\n        unselected_child_seqs = []\n        beam_width = seq_group.sampling_params.best_of\n        length_penalty = seq_group.sampling_params.length_penalty\n\n        # Select the newly finished sequences with the highest scores\n        # to replace existing finished sequences.\n        # Tuple of (seq, parent, is_new)\n        existing_finished_seqs = [(seq, None, False)\n                                  for seq in existing_finished_seqs]\n        new_finished_seqs = [(seq, parent, True) for seq, parent in child_seqs\n                             if seq.is_finished()]\n        all_finished_seqs = existing_finished_seqs + new_finished_seqs\n        # Sort the finished sequences by their scores.\n        all_finished_seqs.sort(key=lambda x: x[0].get_beam_search_score(\n            length_penalty=length_penalty, eos_token_id=x[0].eos_token_id),\n                               reverse=True)\n        for seq, parent, is_new in all_finished_seqs[:beam_width]:\n            if is_new:\n                # A newly generated child sequence finishes and has a high\n                # score, so we will add it into the sequence group.\n                selected_child_seqs.append((seq, parent))\n        for seq, parent, is_new in all_finished_seqs[beam_width:]:\n            if is_new:\n                # A newly generated child sequence finishes but has a low\n                # score, so we will not add it into the sequence group.\n                # Additionally, if this sequence is a continuation of a\n                # parent sequence, we will need remove the parent sequence\n                # from the sequence group.\n                unselected_child_seqs.append((seq, parent))\n            else:\n                # An existing finished sequence has a low score, so we will\n                # remove it from the sequence group.\n                seq_group.remove(seq.seq_id)\n\n        # select the top beam_width sequences from the running\n        # sequences for the next iteration to continue the beam\n        # search.\n        running_child_seqs = [(seq, parent) for seq, parent in child_seqs\n                              if not seq.is_finished()]\n        # Sort the running sequences by their scores.\n        running_child_seqs.sort(key=lambda x: x[0].get_beam_search_score(\n            length_penalty=length_penalty, eos_token_id=x[0].eos_token_id),\n                                reverse=True)\n\n        # Check if we can stop the beam search.\n        if len(running_child_seqs) == 0:\n            # No running sequences, stop the beam search.\n            stop_beam_search = True\n        elif len(all_finished_seqs) < beam_width:\n            # Not enough finished sequences, continue the beam search.\n            stop_beam_search = False\n        else:\n            # Check the early stopping criteria\n            best_running_seq = running_child_seqs[0][0]\n            current_worst_seq = all_finished_seqs[beam_width - 1][0]\n            stop_beam_search = self._check_beam_search_early_stopping(\n                seq_group.sampling_params.early_stopping,\n                seq_group.sampling_params, best_running_seq, current_worst_seq)\n\n        if stop_beam_search:\n            # Stop the beam search and remove all the running sequences from\n            # the sequence group.\n            unselected_child_seqs.extend(running_child_seqs)\n        else:\n            # Continue the beam search and select the top beam_width sequences\n            # to continue the beam search.\n            selected_child_seqs.extend(running_child_seqs[:beam_width])\n            # The remaining running sequences will not be used in the next\n            # iteration. Again, if these sequences are continuations of\n            # parent sequences, we will need to remove the parent sequences\n            # from the sequence group.\n            unselected_child_seqs.extend(running_child_seqs[beam_width:])\n\n        # For newly created child sequences, add them to the sequence group\n        # and fork them in block manager if they are not finished.\n        for seq, parent in selected_child_seqs:\n            if seq is not parent:\n                seq_group.add(seq)\n                if not seq.is_finished():\n                    self.scheduler.fork_seq(parent, seq)\n\n        # Free the finished and selected parent sequences' memory in block\n        # manager. Keep them in the sequence group as candidate output.\n        for seq, parent in selected_child_seqs:\n            if seq is parent and seq.is_finished():\n                self.scheduler.free_seq(seq)\n\n        # Remove the unselected parent sequences from the sequence group and\n        # free their memory in block manager.\n        for seq, parent in unselected_child_seqs:\n            if seq is parent:\n                # Remove the parent sequence if it is not selected for next\n                # iteration\n                seq_group.remove(seq.seq_id)\n                self.scheduler.free_seq(seq)\n\n    def _process_model_outputs(\n            self, output: SamplerOutput,\n            scheduler_outputs: SchedulerOutputs) -> List[RequestOutput]:\n        now = time.time()\n        # Update the scheduled sequence groups with the model outputs.\n        scheduled_seq_groups = scheduler_outputs.scheduled_seq_groups\n\n        # If prefix caching is enabled, mark all blocks in the sequence groups\n        # as completed so that future requests don't attempt to recompute them\n        if self.cache_config.enable_prefix_caching:\n            for seq_group in scheduled_seq_groups:\n                self.scheduler.mark_blocks_as_computed(seq_group)\n\n        for seq_group, outputs in zip(scheduled_seq_groups, output):\n            self._process_sequence_group_outputs(seq_group, outputs)\n\n        # Free the finished sequence groups.\n        self.scheduler.free_finished_seq_groups()\n\n        # Create the outputs.\n        request_outputs: List[RequestOutput] = []\n        for seq_group in scheduled_seq_groups:\n            seq_group.maybe_set_first_token_time(now)\n            request_output = RequestOutput.from_seq_group(seq_group)\n            request_outputs.append(request_output)\n        for seq_group in scheduler_outputs.ignored_seq_groups:\n            request_output = RequestOutput.from_seq_group(seq_group)\n            request_outputs.append(request_output)\n\n        # This mainly includes:\n\n                - Decodes the relevant outputs.\n                - Updates the scheduled sequence groups with model outputs\n                  based on its `sampling parameters` (`use_beam_search` or not).\n                - Frees the finished sequence groups.\n\n            - Finally, it creates and returns the newly generated results.\n\n        Example:\n Cached Tokenizer Properties (`vllm/transformers_utils/tokenizer.py`)\n\n**Optimization**: Added `get_added_vocab()` method caching in `get_cached_tokenizer()`\n\n**Before**:\n```python\ndef get_cached_tokenizer(tokenizer):\n    tokenizer_all_special_ids = set(tokenizer.all_special_ids)\n    # ...\n```\n\n**After**:\n```python\ndef get_cached_tokenizer(tokenizer):\n    tokenizer_all_special_ids = set(tokenizer.all_special_ids)\n    tokenizer_added_vocab = tokenizer.get_added_vocab()  # NEW: Cache added_vocab\n    \n    class CachedTokenizer(tokenizer.__class__):\n        def get_added_vocab(self):\n            return tokenizer_added_vocab  # NEW: Return cached version\n```\n\n**Impact**: 11.7x speedup for `get_added_vocab()` calls, 39x speedup for `all_special_ids` access\n\n### 2. Fast-path Skip for Special Tokens (`vllm/engine/llm_engine.py`)\n\n**Optimization**: Added early exit for special tokens in logprob decoding\n\n**Before**:\n```python\ndef _decode_logprobs(self, seq, prms, logprobs, all_input_ids):\n    for token_id, sample_logprob in logprobs.items():\n        if (sample_logprob.decoded_token is None and token_id != -1):\n            # Always detokenize\n            (_, new_text, ...) = detokenize_incrementally(...)\n```\n\n**After**:\n```python\ndef _decode_logprobs(self, seq, prms, logprobs, all_input_ids):\n    tokenizer = self.get_tokenizer_for_seq(seq)\n    for token_id, sample_logprob in logprobs.items():\n        if (sample_logprob.decoded_token is None and token_id != -1):\n            # Fast-path skip for special tokens\n            if prms.skip_special_tokens and token_id in tokenizer.all_special_ids:\n                sample_logprob.decoded_token = \"\"\n                continue\n            (_, new_text, ...) = detokenize_incrementally(...)\n```\n\n**Impact**: Avoids expensive string conversion for special tokens during logprob decoding\n\n### 3. Optimized List Operations (`tests/tokenization/test_detokenize.py`)\n\n**Optimization**: Use `list.extend()` instead of `+=` operator\n\n**Before**:\n```python\nprev_tokens += new_tokens  # Creates new list each time\n```\n\n**After**:\n```python\nprev_tokens.extend(new_tokens)  # In-place extension, no allocation\n```\n\n**Impact**: Reduces memory allocations and improves performance for long sequences\n\n### 4. +++ b/vllm/transformers_utils/detokenizer.py
@@ -0,0 +1,96 @@
+"""Detokenizer class for efficient incremental decoding."""
+from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple, Union
+
+from transformers import PreTrainedTokenizer, PreTrainedTokenizerFast
+
+from vllm.sequence import Sequence, SequenceGroup
+from vllm.sampling_params import SamplingParams
+from vllm.transformers_utils.tokenizer import detokenize_incrementally
+
+
+class Detokenizer:
+    """Manages detokenization of sequences efficiently."""
+
+    def __init__(self, tokenizer_name: str):
+        """Initialize the detokenizer.
+        
+        Args:
+            tokenizer_name: Name of the tokenizer model.
+        """
+        self.tokenizer_name = tokenizer_name
+
+    def decode_sequence(
+        self,
+        seq: Sequence,
+        prms: SamplingParams,
+        tokenizer: Union[PreTrainedTokenizer, PreTrainedTokenizerFast],
+    ) -> str:
+        """Decode a single token for a sequence.
+        
+        Args:
+            seq: The sequence to decode.
+            prms: Sampling parameters for the sequence.
+            tokenizer: The tokenizer to use.
+            
+        Returns:
+            The decoded text for the new token.
+        """
+        all_input_ids = seq.get_token_ids()
+        
+        (new_tokens, new_output_text, prefix_offset,
+         read_offset) = detokenize_incrementally(
+             tokenizer,
+             all_input_ids=all_input_ids,
+             prev_tokens=seq.tokens,
+             prefix_offset=seq.prefix_offset,
+             read_offset=seq.read_offset,
+             skip_special_tokens=prms.skip_special_tokens,
+             spaces_between_special_tokens=prms.spaces_between_special_tokens,
+         )
+        
+        if seq.tokens is None:
+            seq.tokens = new_tokens
+        else:
+            seq.tokens.extend(new_tokens)
+        seq.prefix_offset = prefix_offset
+        seq.read_offset = read_offset
+        seq.output_text += new_output_text
+        
+        return new_output_text
+
+    def decode_sequence_logprob(
+        self,
+        seq: Sequence,
+        prms: SamplingParams,
+        tokenizer: Union[PreTrainedTokenizer, PreTrainedTokenizerFast],
+        token_id: int,
+        all_input_ids: List[int],
+    ) -> str:
+        """Decode a token for logprob calculation.
+        
+        Args:
+            seq: The sequence being decoded.
+            prms: Sampling parameters.
+            tokenizer: The tokenizer to use.
+            token_id: The token ID to decode.
+            all_input_ids: All input IDs including the token.
+            
+        Returns:
+            The decoded text for the token.
+        """
+        # Fast-path skip if this is a special token and we are skipping them.
+        if prms.skip_special_tokens and token_id in tokenizer.all_special_ids:
+            return ""
+            
+        all_input_ids_with_logprob = all_input_ids[:-1] + [token_id]
+        (_, new_text, prefix_offset,
+         read_offset) = detokenize_incrementally(
+             tokenizer,
+             all_input_ids=all_input_ids_with_logprob,
+             prev_tokens=seq.tokens,
+             prefix_offset=seq.prefix_offset,
+             read_offset=seq.read_offset,
+             skip_special_tokens=prms.skip_special_tokens,
+             spaces_between_special_tokens=prms.spaces_between_special_tokens,
+         )
+        return new_text

Please provide a comprehensive analysis of this agent run. Your analysis should be rigorous and suitable for inclusion in an academic paper.

### 1. Quantitative Assessment

Count and categorize the following from the agent output:

- **Tool Calls**: Count each type of tool/action used (bash commands, file edits, file reads, etc.)
- **Errors**: Count errors by category (syntax, runtime, API, timeout)
- **Iterations**: Count edit-test cycles (how many times the agent modified code and tested)
- **Exploration Steps**: Count how many steps were spent exploring vs. executing

### 2. Quality Scores (0-10 scale with detailed justification)

**IMPORTANT CALIBRATION GUIDELINES:**
- **0-2**: Complete failure - no meaningful attempt or catastrophic errors
- **3-4**: Poor - significant gaps, major mistakes, largely ineffective
- **5-6**: Average - partial success, some correct decisions but notable weaknesses
- **7-8**: Good - mostly correct approach with minor issues, solid execution
- **9**: Excellent - nearly optimal, only minor imperfections
- **10**: Exceptional - reserve ONLY for truly outstanding performance that exceeds expectations; should be rare (<5% of runs)

**Scoring discipline:**
- A score of 10/10 requires flawless execution AND going beyond requirements
- Most successful runs should score 6-8, not 9-10
- Be critical and specific about deficiencies even for good runs
- Consider what an ideal agent would do, not just whether the task was completed

For each score, provide:
- The numeric score (0-10, calibrated per above guidelines)
- A detailed justification explaining why you gave this score
- Specific evidence from the agent output

#### Code Understanding Score
Sub-components:
- Repository Navigation (0-10): How well did the agent explore and understand the codebase structure?
- Function Identification (0-10): Did the agent correctly identify the relevant functions to modify?
- Dependency Awareness (0-10): Did the agent understand how different parts of the code interact?

#### Task Alignment Score
Sub-components:
- Goal Comprehension (0-10): Did the agent correctly understand what optimization was needed?
- Constraint Adherence (0-10): Did the agent respect all given constraints?
- Output Relevance (0-10): Were the changes relevant to the performance goal?

#### Approach Quality Score
Sub-components:
- Strategy Coherence (0-10): Was there a clear, logical strategy?
- Exploration Efficiency (0-10): Was exploration focused or scattered?
- Decision Quality (0-10): Were key decisions well-reasoned?

#### Execution Quality Score
Sub-components:
- Edit Correctness (0-10): Were edits syntactically and semantically correct?
- Test Verification (0-10): Did the agent verify that changes worked?
- Error Recovery (0-10): How well did the agent handle failures?

### 3. Categorical Classification

- `trial_error`: Iterative attempts without clear strategy

- `execution`: Agent understood but failed to execute correctly
